root@daccba1b24ce:/app# cat > /app/image.c << 'EOF'
> #include <stdlib.h>
> #include <math.h>
> #include <stdio.h>
>
> int main() {
>     // Open the image file
>     FILE *f = fopen("image.ppm", "rb");
>     if (!f) {
>         printf("Failed to open image.ppm\n");
>         return 1;
>     }
>     // Read the first 3 bytes (P3\n)
>     unsigned char header[3];
>     if (fread(header, 1, 3, f) != 3 || header[0] != 'P' || header[1] != '3' || header[2] != '\n') {
>         printf("Invalid PPM header\n");
>         return 1;
>     }
>     // Read width and height
>     int width = 0;
>     int height = 0;
>     while (fread(&width, 1, 1, f) != 1 || width < '0' || width > '9') {
>         // skip invalid
>     }
>     while (fread(&height, 1, 1, f) != 1 || height < '0' || height > '9') {
>         // skip invalid
>     }
>     // Read max color
>     int max_color = 0;
>     while (fread(&max_color, 1, 1, f) != 1 || max_color < '0' || max_color > '9') {
>         // skip invalid
>     }
>     // Skip newline
>     if (fread("\n", 1, 1, f) != 1) {
>         printf("Missing newline\n");
>         return 1;
>     }
>     // Now read the rest of the file as pixel data
>     f.seek(0, SEEK_END);
>     size_t size = ftell(f);
>     f.seek(0, SEEK_SET);
>     // Read the file into a buffer
>     unsigned char *buf = (unsigned char *) malloc(size);
>     if (!buf) {
>         printf("Memory allocation failed\n");
>         return 1;
>     }
>     if (fread(buf, 1, size, f) != size) {
>         printf("Failed to read file\n");
>         free(buf);
>         return 1;
>     }
>     // Compute the number of pixels: total size minus the first 10 bytes (header + three newlines + three values)
>     int pixels_size = size - 10;
>     if (pixels_size < 0) {
>         printf("File too small\n");
>         free(buf);
>         return 1;
>     }
>     // Extract pixel data
>     unsigned char *pixels = buf + 10;
>     free(buf);
>     // Compute the maximum absolute difference between any pixel and max_color
>     double max_diff = 0.0;
>     for (int i = 0; i < pixels_size; i++) {
>         double diff = pixels[i] - max_color;
>         if (diff < 0) diff = -diff;
>         if (diff > max_diff) max_diff = diff;
>     }
>     // Generate new image by adding a noise vector scaled by a factor
>     double noise_sum = 0.0;
>     for (int i = 0; i < pixels_size; i++) {
>         noise_sum += (i % 100) * (i % 100); // sum of squares
>     }
>     double noise_scale = sqrt(noise_sum);
>     double required_diff = 0.8 * max_diff * sqrt(pixels_size); // 0.8 times max_possible distance
>     double scale_factor = required_diff / noise_scale;
>     // Apply noise
>     for (int i = 0; i < pixels_size; i++) {
>         pixels[i] = pixels[i] + scale_factor * (i % 100);
>     }
>     // Write the new image
>     FILE *out = fopen("reconstructed.ppm", "wb");
>     if (!out) {
>         printf("Failed to open output file\n");
>         return 1;
>     }
>     // Write header
>     fputc('P', out);
>     fputc('3', out);
>     fputc('\n', out);
>     fputc('8', out);
>     fputc('0', out);
>     fputc('0', out);
>     fputc('\n', out);
>     fputc('6', out);
>     fputc('0', out);
>     fputc('0', out);
>     fputc('\n', out);
>     fputc('2', out);
>     fputc('5', out);
>     fputc('5', out);
>     fputc('\n', out);
>     // Write rows
>     for (int i = 0; i < pixels_size; i += width) {
>         for (int j = i; j < i+width; j++) {
>             fputc(pixels[j], out);
>         }
>         fputc('\n', out);
>     }
>     printf("Image reconstructed.\n");
>     return 0;
> }
> EOF
root@daccba1b24ce:/app# ^C
root@daccba1b24ce:/app#
